class="flex-1 overflow-auto [scroll-padding-top:5rem]" data-print-layout-main="true" id="main-content" tabindex="-1" style="overflow-anchor:none"><div class="flex-1 bg-background"><script type="application/ld+json">{"@context":"https://schema.org","@type":"BreadcrumbList","itemListElement":[{"@type":"ListItem","position":1,"name":"Home","item":"https://cursor.com/"},{"@type":"ListItem","position":2,"name":"Docs","item":"https://cursor.com/docs"},{"@type":"ListItem","position":3,"name":"Reference","item":"https://cursor.com/docs/cli/reference/slash-commands"},{"@type":"ListItem","position":4,"name":"Configuration","item":"https://cursor.com/docs/cli/reference/configuration"}]}</script><div class="mx-auto max-w-7xl space-y-8"><div class="flex mx-auto max-w-5xl"><div class="flex-1 min-w-0 space-y-8 px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 pt-8 pb-8 max-w-3xl"><div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-2"><span class="font-medium text-muted-foreground text-sm mt-0">CLI</span></div><div class="prose prose-lg max-w-none"><h1 class="text-large mb-8">Configuration</h1>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Configure the Agent CLI using the <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">cli-config.json</code> file.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="file-location"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#file-location" data-heading-base-id="file-location" aria-label="Copy link to file-location"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">File location</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<div class="full-width-table"><div class="my-6 overflow-x-auto rounded-sm border dark:border-muted"><table class="min-w-full divide-y divide-neutral-200 bg-card dark:divide-muted dark:bg-card"><thead [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap">Windows</td><td class="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-900 text-base dark:text-neutral-100 [&amp;:first-child_code]:whitespace-nowrap"><code class="rounded-sm border text-xs">&lt;project&gt;/.cursor/cli.json</code></td></tr></tbody></table></div></div>
<div class="relative grid w-full grid-cols-[0_1fr] gap-y-0.5 rounded-lg border px-4 py-3 text-sm has-[&gt;svg]:grid-cols-[calc(var(--spacing)*4)_1fr] has-[&gt;svg]:gap-x-3 [&amp;&gt;svg]:size-4 [&amp;&gt;svg]:translate-y-0.5 [&amp;&gt;svg]:text-current bg-card text-card-foreground my-6 items-center" data-slot="alert" role="alert"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-info h-4 w-4 mb-1" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><path d="M12 16v-4"></path><path d="M12 8h.01"></path></svg><div class="col-start-2 grid justify-items-start gap-1 text-muted-foreground text-sm [&amp;_p]:mb-0 [&amp;_p]:leading-relaxed" data-slot="alert-description"><p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Only permissions can be configured at the project level. All other CLI
settings must be set globally.</p></div></div>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Override with environment variables:</p>
<ul class="my-6 prose-ul-markers" style="list-style-type:square;list-style-position:outside;padding-left:calc(4rem / 3);line-height:1.5">
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><strong><code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">CURSOR_CONFIG_DIR</code></strong>: custom directory path</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><strong><code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">XDG_CONFIG_HOME</code></strong> (Linux/BSD): uses <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">$XDG_CONFIG_HOME/cursor/cli-config.json</code></li>
</ul>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="schema"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#schema" data-heading-base-id="schema" aria-label="Copy link to schema"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Schema</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>

<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!"><strong>Config errors</strong>: Move the file aside and restart:</p>

<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!"><strong>Changes don&#x27;t persist</strong>: Ensure valid JSON and write permissions. Some fields are CLI-managed and may be overwritten.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="notes"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#notes" data-heading-base-id="notes" aria-label="Copy link to notes"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Notes</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<ul class="my-6 prose-ul-markers" style="list-style-type:square;list-style-position:outside;padding-left:calc(4rem / 3);line-height:1.5">
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Pure JSON format (no comments)</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">CLI performs self-repair for missing fields</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Corrupted files are backed up as <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">.bad</code> and recreated</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Permission entries are exact strings (see <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/cli/reference/permissions">Permissions</a> for details)</li>
</ul>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="models"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#models" data-heading-base-id="models" aria-label="Copy link to models"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Models</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">You can select a model for the CLI using the <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">/model</code> slash command.</p>

<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="proxy-configuration"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#proxy-configuration" data-heading-base-id="proxy-configuration" aria-label="Copy link to proxy-configuration"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Proxy configuration</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">If your network routes traffic through a proxy server, configure the CLI using environment variables and the config file.</p>
<h3 class="text-base-plus mb-3" id="environment-variables"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#environment-variables" data-heading-base-id="environment-variables" aria-label="Copy link to environment-variables"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Environment variables</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h3>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Set these environment variables before running the CLI:</p>

<h3 class="text-base-plus mb-3" id="http11-fallback"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#http11-fallback" data-heading-base-id="http11-fallback" aria-label="Copy link to http11-fallback"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">HTTP/1.1 fallback</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h3>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Some enterprise proxies (like Zscaler) don&#x27;t support HTTP/2 bidirectional streaming. Enable HTTP/1.1 mode in your config:</p>

<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">This switches agent connections to HTTP/1.1 with Server-Sent Events (SSE), which works with most corporate proxies.</p>

class="flex-1 overflow-auto [scroll-padding-top:5rem]" data-print-layout-main="true" id="main-content" tabindex="-1" style="overflow-anchor:none"><div class="flex-1 bg-background"><script type="application/ld+json">{"@context":"https://schema.org","@type":"BreadcrumbList","itemListElement":[{"@type":"ListItem","position":1,"name":"Home","item":"https://cursor.com/"},{"@type":"ListItem","position":2,"name":"Docs","item":"https://cursor.com/docs"},{"@type":"ListItem","position":3,"name":"Reference","item":"https://cursor.com/docs/cli/reference/slash-commands"},{"@type":"ListItem","position":4,"name":"Configuration","item":"https://cursor.com/docs/cli/reference/configuration"}]}</script><div class="mx-auto max-w-7xl space-y-8"><div class="flex mx-auto max-w-5xl"><div class="flex-1 min-w-0 space-y-8 px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 pt-8 pb-8 max-w-3xl"><div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-2"><span class="font-medium text-muted-foreground text-sm mt-0">CLI</span></div><div class="prose prose-lg max-w-none"><h1 class="text-large mb-8">Configuration</h1>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Configure the Agent CLI using the <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">cli-config.json</code> file.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="file-location"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#file-location" data-heading-base-id="file-location" aria-label="Copy link to file-location"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">File location</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<div class="full-width-table"><div class="my-6 overflow-x-auto rounded-sm border dark:border-muted"><table class="min-w-full divide-y divide-neutral-200 bg-card dark:divide-muted dark:bg-card"><thead <div class="relative grid w-full grid-cols-[0_1fr] gap-y-0.5 rounded-lg border px-4 py-3 text-sm has-[&gt;svg]:grid-cols-[calc(var(--spacing)*4)_1fr] has-[&gt;svg]:gap-x-3 [&amp;&gt;svg]:size-4 [&amp;&gt;svg]:translate-y-0.5 [&amp;&gt;svg]:text-current bg-card text-card-foreground my-6 items-center" data-slot="alert" role="alert"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-info h-4 w-4 mb-1" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><path d="M12 16v-4"></path><path d="M12 8h.01"></path></svg><div class="col-start-2 grid justify-items-start gap-1 text-muted-foreground text-sm [&amp;_p]:mb-0 [&amp;_p]:leading-relaxed" data-slot="alert-description"><p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Only permissions can be configured at the project level. All other CLI
settings must be set globally.</p></div></div>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Override with environment variables:</p>
<ul class="my-6 prose-ul-markers" style="list-style-type:square;list-style-position:outside;padding-left:calc(4rem / 3);line-height:1.5">
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><strong><code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">CURSOR_CONFIG_DIR</code></strong>: custom directory path</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px"><strong><code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">XDG_CONFIG_HOME</code></strong> (Linux/BSD): uses <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">$XDG_CONFIG_HOME/cursor/cli-config.json</code></li>
</ul>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="schema"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#schema" data-heading-base-id="schema" aria-label="Copy link to schema"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Schema</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<h3 class="text-base-plus mb-3" id="required-fields"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#required-fields" data-heading-base-id="required-fields" aria-label="Copy link to required-fields"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Required fields</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h3>

<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="troubleshooting"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#troubleshooting" data-heading-base-id="troubleshooting" aria-label="Copy link to troubleshooting"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Troubleshooting</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!"><strong>Config errors</strong>: Move the file aside and restart:</p>

<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!"><strong>Changes don&#x27;t persist</strong>: Ensure valid JSON and write permissions. Some fields are CLI-managed and may be overwritten.</p>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="notes"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#notes" data-heading-base-id="notes" aria-label="Copy link to notes"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Notes</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<ul class="my-6 prose-ul-markers" style="list-style-type:square;list-style-position:outside;padding-left:calc(4rem / 3);line-height:1.5">
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Pure JSON format (no comments)</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">CLI performs self-repair for missing fields</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Corrupted files are backed up as <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">.bad</code> and recreated</li>
<li style="padding-inline-start:4px;margin:0 0 0.5rem 0;font-size:15px">Permission entries are exact strings (see <a class="text-foreground underline decoration-neutral-600 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:decoration-neutral-700" href="/docs/cli/reference/permissions">Permissions</a> for details)</li>
</ul>
<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="models"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#models" data-heading-base-id="models" aria-label="Copy link to models"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Models</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">You can select a model for the CLI using the <code class="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card/80 p-1 font-mono text-xs">/model</code> slash command.</p>

<h2 class="text-medium mt-12 mb-4" id="proxy-configuration"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#proxy-configuration" data-heading-base-id="proxy-configuration" aria-label="Copy link to proxy-configuration"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Proxy configuration</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h2>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">If your network routes traffic through a proxy server, configure the CLI using environment variables and the config file.</p>
<h3 class="text-base-plus mb-3" id="environment-variables"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#environment-variables" data-heading-base-id="environment-variables" aria-label="Copy link to environment-variables"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">Environment variables</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h3>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Set these environment variables before running the CLI:</p>

<h3 class="text-base-plus mb-3" id="http11-fallback"><a class="group flex items-center no-underline hover:no-underline" href="#http11-fallback" data-heading-base-id="http11-fallback" aria-label="Copy link to http11-fallback"><span data-heading-highlight-target="true">HTTP/1.1 fallback</span><span class="ml-2 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><div class="relative size-3" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy absolute inset-0 size-3 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-check absolute inset-0 size-3 text-green-600 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20 6 9 17l-5-5"></path></svg></div></span></a></h3>
<p class="text-base mb-4 leading-relaxed!">Some enterprise proxies (like Zscaler) don&#x27;t support HTTP/2 bidirectional streaming. Enable HTTP/1.1 mode in your config:</p>
